- Toggle shortcuts help`?`
- Toggle \"can call user code\" annotations`u`
- Toggle early exit annotations`e`
- Navigate to/from multipage`m`
- Jump to search box`/`
- Toggle pinning of the current clause`p`
- Jump to the *n*^th^ pin`1-9`
- Jump to the 10^th^ pin`0`

Multipage preference

Always multi-page

# [10] Ordinary and Exotic Objects Behaviours

# [10.1] Ordinary Object Internal Methods and Internal Slots

All [ordinary
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object) have an
internal slot called `[[Prototype]]`. The value of
this internal slot is either null or an object and is used for
implementing inheritance. Assume a property named
`propertyKey` is missing from an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` but exists on its `[[Prototype]]`
object. If `propertyKey` refers to a [data
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) on the
`[[Prototype]]` object, `obj` inherits it
for get access, making it behave as if `propertyKey` was a
property of `obj`. If `propertyKey` refers to a
writable [data
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) on the
`[[Prototype]]` object, set access of
`propertyKey` on `obj` creates a new [data
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) named
`propertyKey` on `obj`. If
`propertyKey` refers to a non-writable [data
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) on the
`[[Prototype]]` object, set access of
`propertyKey` on `obj` fails. If
`propertyKey` refers to an [accessor
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) on the
`[[Prototype]]` object, the accessor is inherited by
`obj` for both get access and set access.

Every [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object) has a
Boolean-valued `[[Extensible]]` internal slot which is
used to fulfill the extensibility-related internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) invariants
specified in
[6.1.7.3](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods).
Namely, once the value of an object\'s `[[Extensible]]` internal slot has been set to false, it is no longer possible to
add properties to the object, to modify the value of the object\'s
`[[Prototype]]` internal slot, or to subsequently
change the value of `[[Extensible]]` to true.

In the following algorithm descriptions, assume `obj` is an
[ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object),
`propertyKey` is a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) value,
`value` is any [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types),
and `propertyDesc` is a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
record.

Each [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object) internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) delegates to a
similarly-named abstract operation. If such an abstract operation
depends on another internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method), then the
internal [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) is
invoked on `obj` rather than calling the similarly-named
abstract operation directly. These semantics ensure that [exotic
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object) have their
overridden internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) invoked when
[ordinary object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
internal [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) are
applied to them.

# [10.1.1] `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` ( )

The `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either an Object or null. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinaryGetPrototypeOf](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarygetprototypeof)(`obj`).

# [10.1.1.1] OrdinaryGetPrototypeOf ( `obj` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryGetPrototypeOf takes argument
`obj` (an Object) and returns an Object or null. It performs
the following steps when called:

1. Return `obj`.`[[Prototype]]`.

# [10.1.2] `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` ( `proto` )

The `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes argument `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinarySetPrototypeOf](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarysetprototypeof)(`obj`,
 `proto`).

# [10.1.2.1] OrdinarySetPrototypeOf ( `obj`, `proto` )

The abstract operation OrdinarySetPrototypeOf takes arguments
`obj` (an Object) and `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `current` be
 `obj`.`[[Prototype]]`.
2. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`proto`,
 `current`) is true, return true.
3. Let `extensible` be
 `obj`.`[[Extensible]]`.
4. If `extensible` is false, return false.
5. Let `cursor` be `proto`.
6. Let `done` be false.
7. [Repeat, while `done` is
 false,]
 1. If `cursor` is null, then
 1. Set `done` to true.
 2. Else if
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`cursor`,
 `obj`) is true, then
 1. Return false.
 3. Else,
 1. If
 `cursor`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)
 is not the [ordinary
 object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
 internal
 [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method)
 defined in
 [10.1.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots-getprototypeof),
 set `done` to true.
 2. Else, set `cursor` to
 `cursor`.`[[Prototype]]`.
8. Set `obj`.`[[Prototype]]` to
 `proto`.
9. Return true.

[Note]

::: note-contents
The loop in step
[7](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#step-ordinarysetprototypeof-loop)
guarantees that there will be no cycles in any
[prototype](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#prototype) chain that
only includes objects that use the [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
definitions for `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` and
`[[SetPrototypeOf]]`.

# [10.1.3] `[[IsExtensible]]` ( )

The `[[IsExtensible]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinaryIsExtensible](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryisextensible)(`obj`).

# [10.1.3.1] OrdinaryIsExtensible ( `obj` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryIsExtensible takes argument
`obj` (an Object) and returns a Boolean. It performs the
following steps when called:

1. Return `obj`.`[[Extensible]]`.

# [10.1.4] `[[PreventExtensions]]` ( )

The `[[PreventExtensions]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
true. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinaryPreventExtensions](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarypreventextensions)(`obj`).

# [10.1.4.1] OrdinaryPreventExtensions ( `obj` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryPreventExtensions takes argument
`obj` (an Object) and returns true. It performs the following
steps when called:

1. Set `obj`.`[[Extensible]]` to false.
2. Return true.

# [10.1.5] `[[GetOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[GetOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinaryGetOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarygetownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.1.5.1] OrdinaryGetOwnProperty ( `obj`, `propertyKey` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryGetOwnProperty takes arguments
`obj` (an Object) and `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `obj` does not have an [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) with
 key `propertyKey`, return undefined.
2. Let `propertyDesc` be a newly created [Property
 Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
 with no fields.
3. Let `ownProperty` be `obj`\'s [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) whose
 key is `propertyKey`.
4. If `ownProperty` is a [data
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 then
 1. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Value]]` to
 the value of `ownProperty`\'s `[[Value]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
 2. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 to the value of `ownProperty`\'s
 `[[Writable]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
5. Else,
 1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `ownProperty` is an [accessor
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
 2. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Getter]]` to
 the value of `ownProperty`\'s `[[Getter]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
 3. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Setter]]` to
 the value of `ownProperty`\'s `[[Setter]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
6. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]` to
 the value of `ownProperty`\'s `[[Enumerable]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
7. Set `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`
 to the value of `ownProperty`\'s
 `[[Configurable]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).
8. Return `propertyDesc`.

# [10.1.6] `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc` )

The `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`propertyDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [OrdinaryDefineOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydefineownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc`).

# [10.1.6.1] OrdinaryDefineOwnProperty ( `obj`, `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryDefineOwnProperty takes arguments
`obj` (an Object), `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)), and
`propertyDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `current` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
2. Let `extensible` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`obj`).
3. Return
 [ValidateAndApplyPropertyDescriptor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validateandapplypropertydescriptor)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `extensible`,
 `propertyDesc`, `current`).

# [10.1.6.2] IsCompatiblePropertyDescriptor ( `extensible`, `propertyDesc`, `current` )

The abstract operation IsCompatiblePropertyDescriptor takes arguments
`extensible` (a Boolean), `propertyDesc` (a
[Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)),
and `current` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined) and returns a Boolean. It performs the following steps
when called:

1. Return
 [ValidateAndApplyPropertyDescriptor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validateandapplypropertydescriptor)(undefined,
 \"\", `extensible`, `propertyDesc`,
 `current`).

# [10.1.6.3] ValidateAndApplyPropertyDescriptor ( `obj`, `propertyKey`, `extensible`, `propertyDesc`, `current` )

The abstract operation ValidateAndApplyPropertyDescriptor takes
arguments `obj` (an Object or undefined),
`propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`extensible` (a Boolean), `propertyDesc` (a
[Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)),
and `current` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined) and returns a Boolean. It returns true if and only if
`propertyDesc` can be applied as the property of an object
with specified `extensibility` and current property
`current` while upholding
[invariants](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods).
When such application is possible and `obj` is not undefined,
it is performed for the property named `propertyKey` (which
is created if necessary). It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `propertyKey` is a [property
 key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
2. If `current` is undefined, then
 1. If `extensible` is false, return false.
 2. If `obj` is undefined, return true.
 3. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, then
 1. Create an own [accessor
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type)
 named `propertyKey` of object `obj`
 whose `[[Getter]]`, `[[Setter]]`, `[[Enumerable]]`, and
 `[[Configurable]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to the value of the corresponding field in
 `propertyDesc` if `propertyDesc` has
 that field, or to the
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)\'s
 [default
 value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#table-object-property-attributes)
 otherwise.
 4. Else,
 1. Create an own [data
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type)
 named `propertyKey` of object `obj`
 whose `[[Value]]`,
 `[[Writable]]`, `[[Enumerable]]`, and `[[Configurable]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to the value of the corresponding field in
 `propertyDesc` if `propertyDesc` has
 that field, or to the
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)\'s
 [default
 value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#table-object-property-attributes)
 otherwise.
 5. Return true.
3. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `current`
 is a [fully populated Property
 Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type).
4. If `propertyDesc` does not have any fields, return true.
5. If `current`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Configurable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`
 is true, return false.
 2. If `propertyDesc` has an `[[Enumerable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]` is
 not `current`.`[[Enumerable]]`,
 return false.
 3. If
 [IsGenericDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isgenericdescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is false and
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is not
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`current`),
 return false.
 4. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`current`)
 is true, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Getter]]` field and
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`propertyDesc`.`[[Getter]]`, `current`.`[[Getter]]`) is false, return false.
 2. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Setter]]` field and
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`propertyDesc`.`[[Setter]]`, `current`.`[[Setter]]`) is false, return false.
 5. Else if `current`.`[[Writable]]` is
 false, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Writable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 is true, return false.
 2. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step):
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue) returns
 true for NaN values which may be distinguishable by other
 means. Returning here ensures that any existing property of
 `obj` remains unmodified.
 3. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Value]]` field, return
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`propertyDesc`.`[[Value]]`, `current`.`[[Value]]`).
6. If `obj` is not undefined, then
 1. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`current`)
 is true and
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a
 `[[Configurable]]` field, let
 `configurable` be
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`; else let `configurable` be
 `current`.`[[Configurable]]`.
 2. If `propertyDesc` has an
 `[[Enumerable]]` field, let
 `enumerable` be
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]`; else let `enumerable` be
 `current`.`[[Enumerable]]`.
 3. Replace the property named `propertyKey` of
 object `obj` with an [accessor
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type)
 whose `[[Configurable]]` and
 `[[Enumerable]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to `configurable` and
 `enumerable`, respectively, and whose
 `[[Getter]]` and `[[Setter]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to the value of the corresponding field in
 `propertyDesc` if `propertyDesc` has
 that field, or to the
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)\'s
 [default
 value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#table-object-property-attributes)
 otherwise.
 2. Else if
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`current`)
 is true and
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a
 `[[Configurable]]` field, let
 `configurable` be
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`; else let `configurable` be
 `current`.`[[Configurable]]`.
 2. If `propertyDesc` has an
 `[[Enumerable]]` field, let
 `enumerable` be
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]`; else let `enumerable` be
 `current`.`[[Enumerable]]`.
 3. Replace the property named `propertyKey` of
 object `obj` with a [data
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type)
 whose `[[Configurable]]` and
 `[[Enumerable]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to `configurable` and
 `enumerable`, respectively, and whose
 `[[Value]]` and `[[Writable]]`
 [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 are set to the value of the corresponding field in
 `propertyDesc` if `propertyDesc` has
 that field, or to the
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)\'s
 [default
 value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#table-object-property-attributes)
 otherwise.
 3. Else,
 1. For each field name `fieldName` of
 `propertyDesc`, set the
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute)
 named `fieldName` of the property named
 `propertyKey` of object `obj` to the
 value of `propertyDesc`\'s `fieldName`
 field.
7. Return true.

# [10.1.7] `[[HasProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[HasProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [OrdinaryHasProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryhasproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.1.7.1] OrdinaryHasProperty ( `obj`, `propertyKey` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryHasProperty takes arguments
`obj` (an Object) and `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `hasOwn` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
2. If `hasOwn` is not undefined, return true.
3. Let `parent` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
4. If `parent` is not null, then
 1. Return
 ? [`parent`.[\[\[HasProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-hasproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
5. Return false.

# [10.1.8] `[[Get]]` ( `propertyKey`, `receiver` )

The `[[Get]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [OrdinaryGet](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryget)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `receiver`).

# [10.1.8.1] OrdinaryGet ( `obj`, `propertyKey`, `receiver` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryGet takes arguments `obj` (an
Object), `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)), and
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `propertyDesc` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
2. If `propertyDesc` is undefined, then
 1. Let `parent` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
 2. If `parent` is null, return undefined.
 3. Return
 ? [`parent`.[\[\[Get\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-get)(`propertyKey`,
 `receiver`)].
3. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, return `propertyDesc`.`[[Value]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true.
5. Let `getter` be
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Getter]]`.
6. If `getter` is undefined, return undefined.
7. Return
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`getter`,
 `receiver`).

# [10.1.9] `[[Set]]` ( `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver` )

The `[[Set]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [OrdinarySet](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryset)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `value`,
 `receiver`).

# [10.1.9.1] OrdinarySet ( `obj`, `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver` )

The abstract operation OrdinarySet takes arguments `obj` (an
Object), `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `ownDesc` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
2. Return
 ? [OrdinarySetWithOwnDescriptor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarysetwithowndescriptor)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver`,
 `ownDesc`).

# [10.1.9.2] OrdinarySetWithOwnDescriptor ( `obj`, `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver`, `ownDesc` )

The abstract operation OrdinarySetWithOwnDescriptor takes arguments
`obj` (an Object), `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `ownDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined) and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `ownDesc` is undefined, then
 1. Let `parent` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
 2. If `parent` is not null, return
 ? [`parent`.[\[\[Set\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-set)(`propertyKey`,
 `value`, `receiver`)].
 3. Set `ownDesc` to the PropertyDescriptor {
 `[[Value]]`: undefined,
 `[[Writable]]`: true,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: true,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }.
2. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`ownDesc`)
 is true, then
 1. If `ownDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is
 false, return false.
 2. If `receiver` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 return false.
 3. Let `existingDesc` be
 ? [`receiver`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
 4. If `existingDesc` is undefined, then
 1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `receiver` does not currently have a property
 `propertyKey`.
 2. Return
 ? [CreateDataProperty](abstract-operations.html#sec-createdataproperty)(`receiver`,
 `propertyKey`, `value`).
 5. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`existingDesc`)
 is true, return false.
 6. If `existingDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is
 false, return false.
 7. Let `valueDesc` be the PropertyDescriptor {
 `[[Value]]`: `value` }.
 8. Return
 ? [`receiver`.[\[\[DefineOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-defineownproperty)(`propertyKey`,
 `valueDesc`)].
3. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`ownDesc`)
 is true.
4. Let `setter` be
 `ownDesc`.`[[Setter]]`.
5. If `setter` is undefined, return false.
6. Perform
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`setter`,
 `receiver`, « `value` »).
7. Return true.

# [10.1.10] `[[Delete]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[Delete]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [OrdinaryDelete](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydelete)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.1.10.1] OrdinaryDelete ( `obj`, `propertyKey` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryDelete takes arguments `obj`
(an Object) and `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `propertyDesc` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
2. If `propertyDesc` is undefined, return true.
3. If `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 true, then
 1. Remove the [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property)
 with name `propertyKey` from `obj`.
 2. Return true.
4. Return false.

# [10.1.11] `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` ( )

The `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [property keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 [OrdinaryOwnPropertyKeys](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryownpropertykeys)(`obj`).

# [10.1.11.1] OrdinaryOwnPropertyKeys ( `obj` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryOwnPropertyKeys takes argument
`obj` (an Object) and returns a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [property keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `keys` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
2. For each [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key
 `propertyKey` of `obj` such that
 `propertyKey` is an [array
 index](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#array-index), in
 ascending numeric index order, do
 1. Append `propertyKey` to `keys`.
3. For each [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key
 `propertyKey` of `obj` such that
 `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type)
 and `propertyKey` is not an [array
 index](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#array-index), in
 ascending chronological order of property creation, do
 1. Append `propertyKey` to `keys`.
4. For each [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key
 `propertyKey` of `obj` such that
 `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 in ascending chronological order of property creation, do
 1. Append `propertyKey` to `keys`.
5. Return `keys`.

# [10.1.12] OrdinaryObjectCreate ( `proto` \[ , `additionalInternalSlotsList` \] )

The abstract operation OrdinaryObjectCreate takes argument
`proto` (an Object or null) and optional argument
`additionalInternalSlotsList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of names of internal slots) and returns an Object. It is used to specify
the runtime creation of new [ordinary
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object).
`additionalInternalSlotsList` contains the names of
additional internal slots that must be defined as part of the object,
beyond `[[Prototype]]` and `[[Extensible]]`. If `additionalInternalSlotsList` is not provided, a
new empty
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
is used. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `internalSlotsList` be « `[[Prototype]]`, `[[Extensible]]` ».
2. If `additionalInternalSlotsList` is present, set
 `internalSlotsList` to the
 [list-concatenation](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#list-concatenation)
 of `internalSlotsList` and
 `additionalInternalSlotsList`.
3. Let `obj` be
 [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)(`internalSlotsList`).
4. Set `obj`.`[[Prototype]]` to
 `proto`.
5. Return `obj`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
Although OrdinaryObjectCreate does little more than call
[MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject), its use
communicates the intention to create an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object), and not
an exotic one. Thus, within this specification, it is not called by any
algorithm that subsequently modifies the internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of the object in
ways that would make the result non-ordinary. Operations that create
[exotic objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object)
invoke [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)
directly.

# [10.1.13] OrdinaryCreateFromConstructor ( `ctor`, `intrinsicDefaultProto` \[ , `internalSlotsList` \] )

The abstract operation OrdinaryCreateFromConstructor takes arguments
`ctor` (a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`intrinsicDefaultProto` (a String) and optional argument
`internalSlotsList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of names of internal slots) and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an Object or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It creates an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object) whose
`[[Prototype]]` value is retrieved from a
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)\'s
\"prototype\" property, if it exists, and otherwise is the intrinsic
named by `intrinsicDefaultProto` in the
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)\'s
[realm](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm).
`internalSlotsList` contains the names of additional internal
slots that must be defined as part of the object. It performs the
following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `intrinsicDefaultProto` is this specification\'s name of
 an intrinsic object. The corresponding object must be an intrinsic
 that is intended to be used as the `[[Prototype]]`
 value of an object.
2. Let `proto` be
 ? [GetPrototypeFromConstructor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-getprototypefromconstructor)(`ctor`,
 `intrinsicDefaultProto`).
3. If `internalSlotsList` is present, let `slots`
 be `internalSlotsList`.
4. Else, let `slots` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
5. Return
 [OrdinaryObjectCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate)(`proto`,
 `slots`).

# [10.1.14] GetPrototypeFromConstructor ( `ctor`, `intrinsicDefaultProto` )

The abstract operation GetPrototypeFromConstructor takes arguments
`ctor` (a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`intrinsicDefaultProto` (a String) and returns either a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an Object or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It determines the `[[Prototype]]` value that should be
used for an object constructed using a specific
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor). The
value is retrieved from the
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)\'s
\"prototype\" property, if it exists. Otherwise it is the intrinsic
named by `intrinsicDefaultProto` in the
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)\'s
[realm](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm). It performs
the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `intrinsicDefaultProto` is this specification\'s name of
 an intrinsic object. The corresponding object must be an intrinsic
 that is intended to be used as the `[[Prototype]]`
 value of an object.
2. Let `proto` be
 ? [Get](abstract-operations.html#sec-get-o-p)(`ctor`,
 \"prototype\").
3. If `proto` [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 return `proto`.
4. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): A fallback value
 obtained from `ctor`\'s
 [realm](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm) is
 independent of the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
5. Let `realm` be
 ? [GetFunctionRealm](abstract-operations.html#sec-getfunctionrealm)(`ctor`).
6. Return the value in `realm`.`[[Intrinsics]]` for the field identified by
 `intrinsicDefaultProto`.

# [10.1.15] RequireInternalSlot ( `obj`, `internalSlot` )

The abstract operation RequireInternalSlot takes arguments
`obj` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `internalSlot` (an internal slot name) and returns either
a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
unused or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It throws an exception unless `obj` [is an
Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) and has
the given internal slot. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `obj` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 throw a TypeError exception.
2. If `obj` does not have an `internalSlot`
 internal slot, throw a TypeError exception.
3. Return unused.

# [10.2] ECMAScript Function Objects

ECMAScript [function
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
encapsulate parameterized ECMAScript code closed over a lexical
environment and support the dynamic evaluation of that code. An
ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) is an
[ordinary object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object)
and has the same internal slots and the same internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) as other
[ordinary
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object). The
code of an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) may be
either [strict mode
code](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-strict-mode-code)
([11.2.2](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-strict-mode-code)) or
[non-strict code](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#non-strict-code).
An ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) whose
code is [strict mode
code](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-strict-mode-code) is
called a [strict function]. One whose code is not [strict mode
code](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-strict-mode-code) is
called a [non-strict function].

In addition to `[[Extensible]]` and
`[[Prototype]]`, ECMAScript [function
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) also
have the internal slots listed in [Table
26](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects).

<figure>
<span id="table-27"></span>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Internal Slot</th>
<th>Type</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[Environment]]</var></td>
<td>an <a
href="executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records">Environment
Record</a></td>
<td>The <a
href="executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records">Environment
Record</a> that the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
was closed over. Used as the outer environment when evaluating the code
of the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[PrivateEnvironment]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment
Record</a> or null</td>
<td>The <a
href="executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#privateenvironment-record">PrivateEnvironment
Record</a> for <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names">Private
Names</a> that the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
was closed over. null if this <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
is not syntactically contained within a class. Used as the outer

Record</a> or a <a

Record</a></td>

href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
was created.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[ThisMode]]</var></td>
<td>lexical, strict, or global</td>
<td>Defines how <code>this</code> references are interpreted within the
formal parameters and code body of the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>.
lexical means that <code>this</code> refers to the this value of a
lexically enclosing <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>.
strict means that the this value is used exactly as provided by an
invocation of the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>.
global means that a this value of undefined or null is interpreted as a
reference to the <a href="global-object.html#sec-global-object">global
object</a>, and any other this value is first passed to <a
href="abstract-operations.html#sec-toobject">ToObject</a>.</td>

function</a>, false if this is a <a
href="ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#non-strict-function">non-strict
function</a>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[HomeObject]]</var></td>

uses <code>super</code>, this is the object whose <var
class="field">[[GetPrototypeOf]]</var> provides the object where

</tr>
<tr>

<td>a sequence of Unicode code points</td>
<td>The <a
href="ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-source-text">source
text</a> that defines the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[Fields]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a>
of <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-classfielddefinition-record-specification-type">ClassFieldDefinition
Records</a></td>
<td>If the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
is a class, this is a list of <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Records</a>
representing the non-static fields and corresponding initializers of the
class.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[PrivateMethods]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a>
of <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-privateelement-specification-type">PrivateElements</a></td>
<td>If the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
is a class, this is a list representing the non-static private <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method">methods</a> and
accessors of the class.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[ClassFieldInitializerName]]</var></td>
<td>a String, a Symbol, a <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names">Private
Name</a>, or empty</td>
<td>If the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
is created as the initializer of a class field, the name to use for <a
href="syntax-directed-operations.html#sec-runtime-semantics-namedevaluation">NamedEvaluation</a>
of the field; empty otherwise.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[IsClassConstructor]]</var></td>
<td>a Boolean</td>
<td>Indicates whether the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>
is a class <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor">constructor</a>.
(If true, invoking the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">function</a>'s
<var class="field">[[Call]]</var> will immediately throw a TypeError
exception.)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption>Table 26: Internal Slots of ECMAScript <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object">Function</a>
Objects</figcaption>
</figure>

All ECMAScript [function
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) have the
`[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) defined here.
ECMAScript
[functions](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) that
are also
[constructors](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor) in
addition have the `[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method).

# [10.2.1] `[[Call]]` ( `thisArg`, `argList` )

The `[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an ECMAScript
[function object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
`func` takes arguments `thisArg` (an [ECMAScript
language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `callerContext` be the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
2. Let `calleeContext` be
 [PrepareForOrdinaryCall](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-prepareforordinarycall)(`func`,
 undefined).
3. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `calleeContext` is now the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
4. If `func`.`[[IsClassConstructor]]` is
 true, then
 1. Let `error` be a newly created TypeError object.
 2. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step):
 `error` is created in `calleeContext` with
 `func`\'s associated [Realm
 Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm-record).
 3. Remove `calleeContext` from the [execution context
 stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack)
 and restore `callerContext` as the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
 4. Throw `error`.
5. Perform
 [OrdinaryCallBindThis](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarycallbindthis)(`func`,
 `calleeContext`, `thisArg`).
6. Let `result` be
 [Completion](notational-conventions.html#sec-completion-ao)([OrdinaryCallEvaluateBody](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarycallevaluatebody)(`func`,
 `argList`)).
7. [Remove `calleeContext` from the [execution context
 stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack)
 and restore `callerContext` as the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).]
8. If `result` is a [throw
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type),
 return ? `result`.
9. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `result` is
 a [return
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
10. Return `result`.`[[Value]]`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
When `calleeContext` is removed from the [execution context
stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack)
in step
[7](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#step-call-pop-context-stack)
it must not be destroyed if it is suspended and retained for later
resumption by an accessible Generator.

# [10.2.1.1] PrepareForOrdinaryCall ( `func`, `newTarget` )

The abstract operation PrepareForOrdinaryCall takes arguments
`func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`newTarget` (an Object or undefined) and returns an
[execution
context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-execution-contexts).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `callerContext` be the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
2. Let `calleeContext` be a new [ECMAScript code execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#ecmascript-code-execution-context).
3. Set the
 [Function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) of
 `calleeContext` to `func`.
4. Let `calleeRealm` be
 `func`.`[[Realm]]`.
5. Set the [Realm](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm)
 of `calleeContext` to `calleeRealm`.
6. Set the ScriptOrModule of `calleeContext` to
 `func`.`[[ScriptOrModule]]`.
7. Let `localEnv` be
 [NewFunctionEnvironment](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-newfunctionenvironment)(`func`,
 `newTarget`).
8. Set the LexicalEnvironment of `calleeContext` to
 `localEnv`.
9. Set the VariableEnvironment of `calleeContext` to
 `localEnv`.
10. Set the PrivateEnvironment of `calleeContext` to
 `func`.`[[PrivateEnvironment]]`.
11. If `callerContext` is not already suspended, suspend
 `callerContext`.
12. Push `calleeContext` onto the [execution context
 stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack);
 `calleeContext` is now the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
13. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): Any exception objects
 produced after this point are associated with
 `calleeRealm`.
14. Return `calleeContext`.

# [10.2.1.2] OrdinaryCallBindThis ( `func`, `calleeContext`, `thisArg` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryCallBindThis takes arguments
`func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)),
`calleeContext` (an [execution
context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-execution-contexts)),
and `thisArg` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns unused. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `thisMode` be
 `func`.`[[ThisMode]]`.
2. If `thisMode` is lexical, return unused.
3. Let `calleeRealm` be
 `func`.`[[Realm]]`.
4. Let `localEnv` be the LexicalEnvironment of
 `calleeContext`.
5. If `thisMode` is strict, then
 1. Let `thisValue` be `thisArg`.
6. Else,
 1. If `thisArg` is either undefined or null, then
 1. Let `globalEnv` be
 `calleeRealm`.`[[GlobalEnv]]`.
 2. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `globalEnv` is a [Global Environment
 Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-global-environment-records).
 3. Let `thisValue` be
 `globalEnv`.`[[GlobalThisValue]]`.
 2. Else,
 1. Let `thisValue` be
 ! [ToObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-toobject)(`thisArg`).
 2. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step):
 [ToObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-toobject) produces
 wrapper objects using `calleeRealm`.
7. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `localEnv`
 is a [Function Environment
 Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-function-environment-records).
8. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): The next step never
 returns an [abrupt
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
 because `localEnv`.`[[ThisBindingStatus]]` is not initialized.
9. Perform
 ! [BindThisValue](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-bindthisvalue)(`localEnv`,
 `thisValue`).
10. Return unused.

# [10.2.1.3] Runtime Semantics: EvaluateBody

The [syntax-directed
operation](notational-conventions.html#sec-algorithm-conventions-syntax-directed-operations)
EvaluateBody takes arguments `func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns a [return
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It is defined piecewise over the following productions:

[FunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionBody)
:
[FunctionStatementList](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionStatementList)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateFunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluatefunctionbody)
 of
 [FunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[ConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ConciseBody)
:
[ExpressionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ExpressionBody)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluateconcisebody)
 of
 [ConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ConciseBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[GeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-GeneratorBody)
:
[FunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionBody)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateGeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluategeneratorbody)
 of
 [GeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-GeneratorBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[AsyncGeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody)
:
[FunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionBody)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateAsyncGeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluateasyncgeneratorbody)
 of
 [AsyncGeneratorBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncGeneratorBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[AsyncFunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncFunctionBody)
:
[FunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-FunctionBody)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateAsyncFunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluateasyncfunctionbody)
 of
 [AsyncFunctionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncFunctionBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[AsyncConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncConciseBody)
:
[ExpressionBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ExpressionBody)

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateAsyncConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluateasyncconcisebody)
 of
 [AsyncConciseBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-AsyncConciseBody)
 with arguments `func` and `argList`.

[Initializer](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#prod-Initializer) : =
[AssignmentExpression](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#prod-AssignmentExpression)

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `argList`
 is empty.
2. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `func`.`[[ClassFieldInitializerName]]`
 is not empty.
3. If
 [IsAnonymousFunctionDefinition](syntax-directed-operations.html#sec-isanonymousfunctiondefinition)([AssignmentExpression](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#prod-AssignmentExpression))
 is true, then
 1. Let `value` be
 ? [NamedEvaluation](syntax-directed-operations.html#sec-runtime-semantics-namedevaluation)
 of
 [Initializer](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#prod-Initializer)
 with argument
 `func`.`[[ClassFieldInitializerName]]`.
4. Else,
 1. Let `rhs` be
 ? [Evaluation](syntax-directed-operations.html#sec-evaluation)
 of
 [AssignmentExpression](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#prod-AssignmentExpression).
 2. Let `value` be
 ? [GetValue](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-getvalue)(`rhs`).
5. Return
 [ReturnCompletion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-returncompletion)(`value`).

[Note]

::: note-contents
Even though field initializers constitute a
[function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
boundary, calling
[FunctionDeclarationInstantiation](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-functiondeclarationinstantiation)
does not have any observable effect and so is omitted.

[ClassStaticBlockBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ClassStaticBlockBody)
:
[ClassStaticBlockStatementList](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ClassStaticBlockStatementList)

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `argList`
 is empty.
2. Return
 ? [EvaluateClassStaticBlockBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluateclassstaticblockbody)
 of
 [ClassStaticBlockBody](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#prod-ClassStaticBlockBody)
 with argument `func`.

# [10.2.1.4] OrdinaryCallEvaluateBody ( `func`, `argList` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryCallEvaluateBody takes arguments
`func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns a [return
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [EvaluateBody](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-runtime-semantics-evaluatebody)
 of `func`.`[[ECMAScriptCode]]` with
 arguments `func` and `argList`.

# [10.2.2] `[[Construct]]` ( `argList`, `newTarget` )

The `[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an ECMAScript
[function object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
`func` takes arguments `argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `newTarget` (a
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)) and
returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an Object or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `callerContext` be the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
2. Let `kind` be
 `func`.`[[ConstructorKind]]`.
3. If `kind` is base, then
 1. Let `thisArg` be
 ? [OrdinaryCreateFromConstructor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarycreatefromconstructor)(`newTarget`,
 \"%Object.prototype%\").
4. Let `calleeContext` be
 [PrepareForOrdinaryCall](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-prepareforordinarycall)(`func`,
 `newTarget`).
5. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `calleeContext` is now the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
6. If `kind` is base, then
 1. Perform
 [OrdinaryCallBindThis](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarycallbindthis)(`func`,
 `calleeContext`, `thisArg`).
 2. Let `initializeResult` be
 [Completion](notational-conventions.html#sec-completion-ao)([InitializeInstanceElements](abstract-operations.html#sec-initializeinstanceelements)(`thisArg`,
 `func`)).
 3. If `initializeResult` is an [abrupt
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type),
 then
 1. Remove `calleeContext` from the [execution
 context
 stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack)
 and restore `callerContext` as the [running
 execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
 2. Return ? `initializeResult`.
7. Let `ctorEnv` be the LexicalEnvironment of
 `calleeContext`.
8. Let `result` be
 [Completion](notational-conventions.html#sec-completion-ao)([OrdinaryCallEvaluateBody](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarycallevaluatebody)(`func`,
 `argList`)).
9. Remove `calleeContext` from the [execution context
 stack](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#execution-context-stack)
 and restore `callerContext` as the [running execution
 context](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#running-execution-context).
10. If `result` is a [throw
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type),
 return ? `result`.
11. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `result` is
 a [return
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
12. If `result`.`[[Value]]` [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 return `result`.`[[Value]]`.
13. If `kind` is base, return `thisArg`.
14. If `result`.`[[Value]]` is not
 undefined, throw a TypeError exception.
15. Let `thisBinding` be
 ? `ctorEnv`.[GetThisBinding](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#abstract-getthisbinding)().
16. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `thisBinding` [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
17. Return `thisBinding`.

# [10.2.3] OrdinaryFunctionCreate ( `proto`, `sourceText`, `paramList`, `body`, `thisMode`, `envRecord`, `privateEnv` )

The abstract operation OrdinaryFunctionCreate takes arguments
`proto` (an Object), `sourceText` (a sequence of
Unicode code points), `paramList` (a [Parse
Node](notational-conventions.html#sec-parsing)), `body` (a
[Parse Node](notational-conventions.html#sec-parsing)),
`thisMode` (lexical-this or non-lexical-this),
`envRecord` (an [Environment
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records)),
and `privateEnv` (a [PrivateEnvironment
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#privateenvironment-record)
or null) and returns an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object). It is
used to specify the runtime creation of a new
[function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) with a
default `[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) and no
`[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) (although one may
be subsequently added by an operation such as
[MakeConstructor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-makeconstructor)).
`sourceText` is the source text of the syntactic definition
of the [function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
to be created. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `internalSlotsList` be the internal slots listed in
 [Table
 26](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-internal-slots-of-ecmascript-function-objects).
2. Let `func` be
 [OrdinaryObjectCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate)(`proto`,
 `internalSlotsList`).
3. Set
 `func`.[\[\[Call\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-call)
 to the definition specified in
 [10.2.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ecmascript-function-objects-call-thisargument-argumentslist).
4. Set `func`.`[[SourceText]]` to
 `sourceText`.
5. Set `func`.`[[FormalParameters]]` to
 `paramList`.
6. Set `func`.`[[ECMAScriptCode]]` to
 `body`.
7. Let `strict` be
 [IsStrict](ecmascript-language-source-code.html#sec-isstrict)(`body`).
8. Set `func`.`[[Strict]]` to
 `strict`.
9. If `thisMode` is lexical-this, set
 `func`.`[[ThisMode]]` to lexical.
10. Else if `strict` is true, set
 `func`.`[[ThisMode]]` to strict.
11. Else, set `func`.`[[ThisMode]]` to
 global.
12. Set `func`.`[[IsClassConstructor]]` to
 false.
13. Set `func`.`[[Environment]]` to
 `envRecord`.
14. Set `func`.`[[PrivateEnvironment]]` to
 `privateEnv`.
15. Set `func`.`[[ScriptOrModule]]` to
 [GetActiveScriptOrModule](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-getactivescriptormodule)().
16. Set `func`.`[[Realm]]` to [the current
 Realm
 Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#current-realm).
17. Set `func`.`[[HomeObject]]` to
 undefined.
18. Set `func`.`[[Fields]]` to a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
19. Set `func`.`[[PrivateMethods]]` to a
 new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
20. Set `func`.`[[ClassFieldInitializerName]]` to empty.
21. Let `length` be the
 [ExpectedArgumentCount](ecmascript-language-functions-and-classes.html#sec-static-semantics-expectedargumentcount)
 of `paramList`.
22. Perform
 [SetFunctionLength](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-setfunctionlength)(`func`,
 `length`).
23. Return `func`.

# [10.2.4] AddRestrictedFunctionProperties ( `func`, `realm` )

The abstract operation AddRestrictedFunctionProperties takes arguments
`func` (a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`realm` (a [Realm
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm-record)) and
returns unused. It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `realm`.`[[Intrinsics]]`.\[\[[%ThrowTypeError%](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-%throwtypeerror%)\]\]
 exists and has been initialized.
2. Let `thrower` be
 `realm`.`[[Intrinsics]]`.\[\[[%ThrowTypeError%](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-%throwtypeerror%)\]\].
3. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`func`,
 \"caller\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Getter]]`:
 `thrower`, `[[Setter]]`:
 `thrower`, `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
4. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`func`,
 \"arguments\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Getter]]`:
 `thrower`, `[[Setter]]`:
 `thrower`, `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
5. Return unused.

# [10.2.4.1] %ThrowTypeError% ( )

This [function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
is the [%ThrowTypeError%] intrinsic object.

It is an anonymous [built-in
function](ecmascript-standard-built-in-objects.html#built-in-function)
object that is defined once for each
[realm](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#realm).

It performs the following steps when called:

1. Throw a TypeError exception.

The value of the `[[Extensible]]` internal slot of
this [function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)
is false.

The \"length\" property of this
[function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) has
the [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute) {
`[[Writable]]`: false, `[[Enumerable]]`: false, `[[Configurable]]`: false }.

The \"name\" property of this
[function](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) has
the [attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute) {
`[[Writable]]`: false, `[[Enumerable]]`: false, `[[Configurable]]`: false }.

# [10.2.5] MakeConstructor ( `func` \[ , `writableProto` \[ , `proto` \] \] )

The abstract operation MakeConstructor takes argument `func`
(an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object) or a
[built-in
function](ecmascript-standard-built-in-objects.html#built-in-function)
object) and optional arguments `writableProto` (a Boolean)
and `proto` (an Object) and returns unused. It converts
`func` into a
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor). It
performs the following steps when called:

1. If `func` is an ECMAScript [function
 object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object), then
 1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsConstructor](abstract-operations.html#sec-isconstructor)(`func`)
 is false.
 2. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `func`
 is an extensible object that does not have a \"prototype\" [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property).
 3. Set
 `func`.[\[\[Construct\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-construct)
 to the definition specified in
 [10.2.2](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ecmascript-function-objects-construct-argumentslist-newtarget).
2. Else,
 1. Set
 `func`.[\[\[Construct\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-construct)
 to the definition specified in
 [10.3.2](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-built-in-function-objects-construct-argumentslist-newtarget).
3. Set `func`.`[[ConstructorKind]]` to
 base.
4. If `writableProto` is not present, set
 `writableProto` to true.
5. If `proto` is not present, then
 1. Set `proto` to
 [OrdinaryObjectCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate)([%Object.prototype%](fundamental-objects.html#sec-properties-of-the-object-prototype-object)).
 2. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`proto`,
 \"constructor\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`: `func`, `[[Writable]]`:
 `writableProto`, `[[Enumerable]]`:
 false, `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
6. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`func`,
 \"prototype\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 `proto`, `[[Writable]]`:
 `writableProto`, `[[Enumerable]]`:
 false, `[[Configurable]]`: false }).
7. Return unused.

# [10.2.6] MakeClassConstructor ( `func` )

The abstract operation MakeClassConstructor takes argument
`func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
returns unused. It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `func`.`[[IsClassConstructor]]` is
 false.
2. Set `func`.`[[IsClassConstructor]]` to
 true.
3. Return unused.

# [10.2.7] MakeMethod ( `func`, `homeObj` )

The abstract operation MakeMethod takes arguments `func` (an
ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`homeObj` (an Object) and returns unused. It configures
`func` as a
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method). It performs the
following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `homeObj`
 is an [ordinary
 object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object).
2. Set `func`.`[[HomeObject]]` to
 `homeObj`.
3. Return unused.

# [10.2.8] DefineMethodProperty ( `homeObj`, `name`, `closure`, `enumerable` )

The abstract operation DefineMethodProperty takes arguments
`homeObj` (an Object), `name` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) or [Private
Name](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names)),
`closure` (a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)), and
`enumerable` (a Boolean) and returns either a [normal
completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either a
[PrivateElement](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-privateelement-specification-type)
or empty, or an [abrupt
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `homeObj`
 is an ordinary, extensible object.
2. If `name` is a [Private
 Name](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names),
 return
 [PrivateElement](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-privateelement-specification-type)
 { `[[Key]]`: `name`,
 `[[Kind]]`: method, `[[Value]]`:
 `closure` }.
3. Let `propertyDesc` be the PropertyDescriptor {
 `[[Value]]`: `closure`,
 `[[Writable]]`: true, `[[Enumerable]]`: `enumerable`, `[[Configurable]]`: true }.
4. Perform
 ? [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`homeObj`,
 `name`, `propertyDesc`).
5. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step):
 [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)
 only returns an [abrupt
 completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
 when attempting to define a class static
 [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) whose
 `name` is \"prototype\".
6. Return empty.

# [10.2.9] SetFunctionName ( `func`, `name` \[ , `prefix` \] )

The abstract operation SetFunctionName takes arguments `func`
(a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`name` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) or [Private
Name](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names)) and
optional argument `prefix` (a String) and returns unused. It
adds a \"name\" property to `func`. It performs the following
steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `func` is
 an extensible object that does not have a \"name\" [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property).
2. If `name` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 then
 1. Let `description` be
 `name`.`[[Description]]`.
 2. If `description` is undefined, set `name`
 to the empty String.
 3. Else, set `name` to the
 [string-concatenation](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#string-concatenation)
 of \"\[\", `description`, and \"\]\".
3. Else if `name` is a [Private
 Name](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-private-names), then
 1. Set `name` to
 `name`.`[[Description]]`.
4. Let `initialName` be `name`.
5. If `prefix` is present, then
 1. Let `prefixedName` be the
 [string-concatenation](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#string-concatenation)
 of `prefix`, the code unit 0x0020 (SPACE), and
 `name`.

 2. Set `initialName` to `prefixedName`.

 3. ::::
 ::: attributes-tag
 [Normative Optional](conformance.html#sec-conformance),
 [Legacy](conformance.html#sec-conformance)
 :::

 Set `initialName` to an
 [implementation-defined](overview.html#implementation-defined)
 choice of either `name` or `prefixedName`.
 ::::

 4. Set `name` to `prefixedName`.
6. If `func` has an `[[InitialName]]`
 internal slot, set `func`.`[[InitialName]]` to `initialName`.
7. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`func`,
 \"name\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 `name`, `[[Writable]]`: false,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
8. Return unused.

[Note]

::: note-contents
The choice of whether or not to use `prefixedName` for
`func`.`[[InitialName]]` affects the output
of [Function.prototype.toString (
)](fundamental-objects.html#sec-function.prototype.tostring). Making
different choices for different [built-in
functions](ecmascript-standard-built-in-objects.html#built-in-function)
in step
[5.c](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#step-setfunctionname-maybe-prefix-initialname)
is discouraged legacy behaviour.

# [10.2.10] SetFunctionLength ( `func`, `length` )

The abstract operation SetFunctionLength takes arguments
`func` (a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)) and
`length` (a non-negative
[integer](notational-conventions.html#integer) or +∞) and returns
unused. It adds a \"length\" property to `func`. It performs
the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `func` is
 an extensible object that does not have a \"length\" [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property).
2. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`func`,
