It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `map` be
 `args`.`[[ParameterMap]]`.
2. Let `isMapped` be
 ! [HasOwnProperty](abstract-operations.html#sec-hasownproperty)(`map`,
 `propertyKey`).
3. Let `result` be
 ? [OrdinaryDelete](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydelete)(`args`,
 `propertyKey`).
4. If `result` is true and `isMapped` is true,
 then
 1. Perform
 ! `map`.[\[\[Delete\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-delete)(`propertyKey`).
5. Return `result`.

# [10.4.4.6] CreateUnmappedArgumentsObject ( `argList` )

The abstract operation CreateUnmappedArgumentsObject takes argument
`argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns an [ordinary
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object). It
performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `length` be the number of elements in
 `argList`.
2. Let `obj` be
 [OrdinaryObjectCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate)([%Object.prototype%](fundamental-objects.html#sec-properties-of-the-object-prototype-object),
 « `[[ParameterMap]]` »).
3. Set `obj`.`[[ParameterMap]]` to
 undefined.
4. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 \"length\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 [𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`length`),
 `[[Writable]]`: true, `[[Enumerable]]`: false, `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
5. Let `index` be 0.
6. Repeat, while `index` \< `length`,
 1. Let `value` be
 `argList`\[`index`\].
 2. Perform
 ! [CreateDataPropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-createdatapropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 ! [ToString](abstract-operations.html#sec-tostring)([𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`index`)),
 `value`).
 3. Set `index` to `index` + 1.
7. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 [%Symbol.iterator%](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-well-known-symbols),
 PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 %Array.prototype.values%, `[[Writable]]`: true,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
8. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 \"callee\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Getter]]`:
 [%ThrowTypeError%](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-%throwtypeerror%),
 `[[Setter]]`:
 [%ThrowTypeError%](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-%throwtypeerror%),
 `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: false }).
9. Return `obj`.

# [10.4.4.7] CreateMappedArgumentsObject ( `func`, `formals`, `argList`, `envRecord` )

The abstract operation CreateMappedArgumentsObject takes arguments
`func` (an ECMAScript [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object)),
`formals` (a [Parse
Node](notational-conventions.html#sec-parsing)), `argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `envRecord` (an [Environment
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records))
and returns an [arguments exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#arguments-exotic-object).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `formals`
 does not contain a rest parameter, any binding patterns, or any
 initializers. It may contain duplicate identifiers.
2. Let `length` be the number of elements in
 `argList`.
3. Let `obj` be
 [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)(«
 `[[Prototype]]`, `[[Extensible]]`, `[[ParameterMap]]` »).
4. Set
 `obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)
 as specified in
 [10.4.4.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-arguments-exotic-objects-getownproperty-p).
5. Set
 `obj`.[\[\[DefineOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-defineownproperty)
 as specified in
 [10.4.4.2](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-arguments-exotic-objects-defineownproperty-p-desc).
6. Set
 `obj`.[\[\[Get\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-get)
 as specified in
 [10.4.4.3](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-arguments-exotic-objects-get-p-receiver).
7. Set
 `obj`.[\[\[Set\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-set)
 as specified in
 [10.4.4.4](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-arguments-exotic-objects-set-p-v-receiver).
8. Set
 `obj`.[\[\[Delete\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-delete)
 as specified in
 [10.4.4.5](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-arguments-exotic-objects-delete-p).
9. Set `obj`.`[[Prototype]]` to
 [%Object.prototype%](fundamental-objects.html#sec-properties-of-the-object-prototype-object).
10. Let `map` be
 [OrdinaryObjectCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryobjectcreate)(null).
11. Set `obj`.`[[ParameterMap]]` to
 `map`.
12. Let `paramNames` be the
 [BoundNames](syntax-directed-operations.html#sec-static-semantics-boundnames)
 of `formals`.
13. Let `paramCount` be the number of elements in
 `paramNames`.
14. Let `index` be 0.
15. Repeat, while `index` \< `length`,
 1. Let `value` be
 `argList`\[`index`\].
 2. Perform
 ! [CreateDataPropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-createdatapropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 ! [ToString](abstract-operations.html#sec-tostring)([𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`index`)),
 `value`).
 3. Set `index` to `index` + 1.
16. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 \"length\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 [𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`length`),
 `[[Writable]]`: true, `[[Enumerable]]`: false, `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
17. Let `mappedNames` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
18. Set `index` to `paramCount` - 1.
19. Repeat, while `index` ≥ 0,
 1. Let `name` be
 `paramNames`\[`index`\].
 2. If `mappedNames` does not contain `name`,
 then
 1. Append `name` to `mappedNames`.
 2. If `index` \< `length`, then
 1. Let `getter` be
 [MakeArgGetter](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-makearggetter)(`name`,
 `envRecord`).
 2. Let `setter` be
 [MakeArgSetter](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-makeargsetter)(`name`,
 `envRecord`).
 3. Perform
 ! `map`.[\[\[DefineOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-defineownproperty)(!
 [ToString](abstract-operations.html#sec-tostring)([𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`index`)),
 PropertyDescriptor { `[[Setter]]`:
 `setter`, `[[Getter]]`:
 `getter`, `[[Enumerable]]`:
 false, `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
 3. Set `index` to `index` - 1.
20. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 [%Symbol.iterator%](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-well-known-symbols),
 PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 %Array.prototype.values%, `[[Writable]]`: true,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
21. Perform
 ! [DefinePropertyOrThrow](abstract-operations.html#sec-definepropertyorthrow)(`obj`,
 \"callee\", PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 `func`, `[[Writable]]`: true,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: false,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }).
22. Return `obj`.

# [10.4.4.7.1] MakeArgGetter ( `name`, `envRecord` )

The abstract operation MakeArgGetter takes arguments `name`
(a String) and `envRecord` (an [Environment
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records))
and returns a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object). It
creates a [built-in
function](ecmascript-standard-built-in-objects.html#built-in-function)
object that when executed returns the value bound for `name`
in `envRecord`. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `getterClosure` be a new [Abstract
 Closure](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-abstract-closure)
 with no parameters that captures `name` and
 `envRecord` and performs the following steps when called:
 1. Return
 [NormalCompletion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-normalcompletion)(!
 `envRecord`.[GetBindingValue](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#abstract-getbindingvalue)(`name`,
 false)).
2. Let `getter` be
 [CreateBuiltinFunction](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-createbuiltinfunction)(`getterClosure`,
 0, \"\", « »).
3. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): `getter`
 is never directly accessible to ECMAScript code.
4. Return `getter`.

# [10.4.4.7.2] MakeArgSetter ( `name`, `envRecord` )

The abstract operation MakeArgSetter takes arguments `name`
(a String) and `envRecord` (an [Environment
Record](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#sec-environment-records))
and returns a [function
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#function-object). It
creates a [built-in
function](ecmascript-standard-built-in-objects.html#built-in-function)
object that when executed sets the value bound for `name` in
`envRecord`. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `setterClosure` be a new [Abstract
 Closure](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-abstract-closure)
 with parameters (`value`) that captures `name`
 and `envRecord` and performs the following steps when
 called:
 1. Return
 [NormalCompletion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-normalcompletion)(!
 `envRecord`.[SetMutableBinding](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#abstract-setmutablebinding)(`name`,
 `value`, false)).
2. Let `setter` be
 [CreateBuiltinFunction](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-createbuiltinfunction)(`setterClosure`,
 1, \"\", « »).
3. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): `setter`
 is never directly accessible to ECMAScript code.
4. Return `setter`.

# [10.4.5] TypedArray Exotic Objects

A [TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
is an [exotic
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object) that
performs special handling of [property
keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) that are
[canonical numeric
strings](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring),
using the subset that are in-bounds [integer
indices](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#integer-index) to index
elements of uniform type and enforcing the invariant that the remainder
are absent without incurring
[prototype](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#prototype) chain
traversal.

[Note]

::: note-contents
Because
[ToString](abstract-operations.html#sec-tostring)(`n`) for
any Number `n` is a [canonical numeric
string](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring), an
implementation may treat Numbers as [property
keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) for
[TypedArrays](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
without actually performing the string conversion.

[TypedArrays](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
have the same internal slots as [ordinary
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#ordinary-object) and
additionally `[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`,
`[[TypedArrayName]]`, `[[ContentType]]`, `[[ByteLength]]`, `[[ByteOffset]]`, and `[[ArrayLength]]` internal slots.

An object is a
[TypedArray] if its
`[[PreventExtensions]]`,
`[[GetOwnProperty]]`, `[[HasProperty]]`, `[[DefineOwnProperty]]`, `[[Get]]`, `[[Set]]`, `[[Delete]]`,
and `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]`, internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) use the
definitions in this section, and its other essential internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) use the
definitions found in
[10.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots).
These [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) are
installed by
[TypedArrayCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraycreate).

# [10.4.5.1] `[[PreventExtensions]]` ( )

The `[[PreventExtensions]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): The
 extensibility-related invariants specified in
 [6.1.7.3](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods)
 do not allow this
 [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) to return
 true when `obj` can gain (or lose and then regain)
 properties, which might occur for properties with [integer
 index](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#integer-index) names
 when its underlying buffer is resized.
2. If
 [IsTypedArrayFixedLength](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayfixedlength)(`obj`)
 is false, return false.
3. Return
 [OrdinaryPreventExtensions](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarypreventextensions)(`obj`).

# [10.4.5.2] `[[GetOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[GetOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, then
 1. Let `value` be
 [TypedArrayGetElement](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraygetelement)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`).
 2. If `value` is undefined, return undefined.
 3. Return the PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`: `value`, `[[Writable]]`: true, `[[Enumerable]]`: true,
 `[[Configurable]]`: true }.
2. Return
 [OrdinaryGetOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarygetownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.4.5.3] `[[HasProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[HasProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, return
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`).
2. Return
 ? [OrdinaryHasProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryhasproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.4.5.4] `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc` )

The `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`propertyDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, then
 1. If
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`) is false, return false.
 2. If `propertyDesc` has a
 `[[Configurable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is false, return false.
 3. If `propertyDesc` has an
 `[[Enumerable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]`
 is false, return false.
 4. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, return false.
 5. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Writable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 is false, return false.
 6. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Value]]` field, perform
 ? [TypedArraySetElement](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraysetelement)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`,
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Value]]`).
 7. Return true.
2. Return
 ! [OrdinaryDefineOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydefineownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc`).

# [10.4.5.5] `[[Get]]` ( `propertyKey`, `receiver` )

The `[[Get]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, then
 1. Return
 [TypedArrayGetElement](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraygetelement)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`).
2. Return
 ? [OrdinaryGet](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryget)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `receiver`).

# [10.4.5.6] `[[Set]]` ( `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver` )

The `[[Set]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, then
 1. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`obj`,
 `receiver`) is true, then
 1. Perform
 ? [TypedArraySetElement](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraysetelement)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`, `value`).
 2. Return true.
 2. If
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`) is false, return true.
2. Return
 ? [OrdinarySet](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryset)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `value`,
 `receiver`).

# [10.4.5.7] `[[Delete]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[Delete]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type),
 then
 1. Let `numericIndex` be
 [CanonicalNumericIndexString](abstract-operations.html#sec-canonicalnumericindexstring)(`propertyKey`).
 2. If `numericIndex` is not undefined, then
 1. If
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `numericIndex`) is false, return true.
 2. Return false.
2. Return
 ! [OrdinaryDelete](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydelete)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).

# [10.4.5.8] `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` ( )

The `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [property keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `taRecord` be
 [MakeTypedArrayWithBufferWitnessRecord](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-maketypedarraywithbufferwitnessrecord)(`obj`,
 seq-cst).
2. Let `keys` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
3. If
 [IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayoutofbounds)(`taRecord`)
 is false, then
 1. Let `length` be
 [TypedArrayLength](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraylength)(`taRecord`).
 2. For each [integer](notational-conventions.html#integer)
 `i` such that 0 ≤ `i` \<
 `length`, in ascending order, do
 1. Append
 ! [ToString](abstract-operations.html#sec-tostring)([𝔽](notational-conventions.html#%F0%9D%94%BD)(`i`))
 to `keys`.
4. For each [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key
 `propertyKey` of `obj` such that
 `propertyKey` [is a
 String](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-string-type)
 and `propertyKey` is not an [integer
 index](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#integer-index), in
 ascending chronological order of property creation, do
 1. Append `propertyKey` to `keys`.
5. For each [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key
 `propertyKey` of `obj` such that
 `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 in ascending chronological order of property creation, do
 1. Append `propertyKey` to `keys`.
6. Return `keys`.

# [10.4.5.9] TypedArray With Buffer Witness Records

An [TypedArray With Buffer Witness Record] is a
[Record](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
used to encapsulate a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
along with a cached byte length of the viewed buffer. It is used to help
ensure there is a single
[ReadSharedMemory](memory-model.html#sec-memory-model-fundamentals)
event of the byte length data block when the viewed buffer is a
[growable
SharedArrayBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-fixed-length-and-growable-sharedarraybuffer-objects).

TypedArray With Buffer Witness Records have the fields listed in [Table
28](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-record-fields).

<figure>
<span
id="table-integer-indexed-object-with-buffer-witness-record-fields"></span>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field Name</th>
<th>Value</th>
<th>Meaning</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[Object]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray">TypedArray</a></td>
<td>The <a
href="ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray">TypedArray</a>
whose buffer's byte length is loaded.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[CachedBufferByteLength]]</var></td>
<td>a non-negative <a
href="notational-conventions.html#integer">integer</a> or detached</td>
<td>The byte length of the object's <var
class="field">[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]</var> when the <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">Record</a>
was created.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption>Table 28: <a
href="ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records">TypedArray
With Buffer Witness Record</a> Fields</figcaption>
</figure>

# [10.4.5.10] MakeTypedArrayWithBufferWitnessRecord ( `obj`, `order` )

The abstract operation MakeTypedArrayWithBufferWitnessRecord takes
arguments `obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray))
and `order` (seq-cst or unordered) and returns a [TypedArray
With Buffer Witness
Record](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `buffer` be
 `obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`.
2. If
 [IsDetachedBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-isdetachedbuffer)(`buffer`)
 is true, then
 1. Let `byteLength` be detached.
3. Else,
 1. Let `byteLength` be
 [ArrayBufferByteLength](structured-data.html#sec-arraybufferbytelength)(`buffer`,
 `order`).
4. Return the [TypedArray With Buffer Witness
 Record](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records)
 { `[[Object]]`: `obj`,
 `[[CachedBufferByteLength]]`:
 `byteLength` }.

# [10.4.5.11] TypedArrayCreate ( `proto` )

The abstract operation TypedArrayCreate takes argument
`proto` (an Object) and returns a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray). It
is used to specify the creation of new
[TypedArrays](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `internalSlotsList` be « `[[Prototype]]`, `[[Extensible]]`,
 `[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`,
 `[[TypedArrayName]]`, `[[ContentType]]`, `[[ByteLength]]`,
 `[[ByteOffset]]`, `[[ArrayLength]]` ».
2. Let `ta` be
 [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)(`internalSlotsList`).
3. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[PreventExtensions\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-preventextensions)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-preventextensions).
4. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.2](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-getownproperty).
5. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[HasProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-hasproperty)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.3](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-hasproperty).
6. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[DefineOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-defineownproperty)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.4](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-defineownproperty).
7. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[Get\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-get)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.5](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-get).
8. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[Set\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-set)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.6](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-set).
9. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[Delete\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-delete)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.7](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-delete).
10. Set
 `ta`.[\[\[OwnPropertyKeys\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-ownpropertykeys)
 as specified in
 [10.4.5.8](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-ownpropertykeys).
11. Set `ta`.`[[Prototype]]` to
 `proto`.
12. Return `ta`.

# [10.4.5.12] TypedArrayByteLength ( `taRecord` )

The abstract operation TypedArrayByteLength takes argument
`taRecord` (a [TypedArray With Buffer Witness
Record](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records))
and returns a non-negative
[integer](notational-conventions.html#integer). It performs the
following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayoutofbounds)(`taRecord`)
 is false.
2. Let `obj` be `taRecord`.`[[Object]]`.
3. If `obj`.`[[ByteLength]]` is not auto,
 return `obj`.`[[ByteLength]]`.
4. Let `length` be
 [TypedArrayLength](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraylength)(`taRecord`).
5. Let `elementSize` be
 [TypedArrayElementSize](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementsize)(`obj`).
6. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): The returned byte
 length is always an [integer](notational-conventions.html#integer)
 multiple of `elementSize`, even when the underlying
 buffer has been resized to a
 non-[integer](notational-conventions.html#integer) multiple.
7. Return `length` × `elementSize`.

# [10.4.5.13] TypedArrayLength ( `taRecord` )

The abstract operation TypedArrayLength takes argument
`taRecord` (a [TypedArray With Buffer Witness
Record](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records))
and returns a non-negative
[integer](notational-conventions.html#integer). It performs the
following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayoutofbounds)(`taRecord`)
 is false.
2. Let `obj` be `taRecord`.`[[Object]]`.
3. If `obj`.`[[ArrayLength]]` is not auto,
 return `obj`.`[[ArrayLength]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsFixedLengthArrayBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-isfixedlengtharraybuffer)(`obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`) is false.
5. Let `byteOffset` be
 `obj`.`[[ByteOffset]]`.
6. Let `elementSize` be
 [TypedArrayElementSize](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementsize)(`obj`).
7. Let `byteLength` be
 `taRecord`.`[[CachedBufferByteLength]]`.
8. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `byteLength` is not detached.
9. Return
 [floor](notational-conventions.html#eqn-floor)((`byteLength` -
 `byteOffset`) / `elementSize`).

# [10.4.5.14] IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds ( `taRecord` )

The abstract operation IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds takes argument
`taRecord` (a [TypedArray With Buffer Witness
Record](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarray-with-buffer-witness-records))
and returns a Boolean. It checks if any of the object\'s numeric
properties reference a value at an index not contained within the
underlying buffer\'s bounds. It performs the following steps when
called:

1. Let `obj` be `taRecord`.`[[Object]]`.
2. Let `bufferByteLength` be
 `taRecord`.`[[CachedBufferByteLength]]`.
3. If
 [IsDetachedBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-isdetachedbuffer)(`obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`) is true, then
 1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `bufferByteLength` is detached.
 2. Return true.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `bufferByteLength` is a non-negative
 [integer](notational-conventions.html#integer).
5. Let `byteOffsetStart` be
 `obj`.`[[ByteOffset]]`.
6. If `obj`.`[[ArrayLength]]` is auto,
 then
 1. Let `byteOffsetEnd` be `bufferByteLength`.
7. Else,
 1. Let `elementSize` be
 [TypedArrayElementSize](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementsize)(`obj`).
 2. Let `arrayByteLength` be
 `obj`.`[[ArrayLength]]` ×
 `elementSize`.
 3. Let `byteOffsetEnd` be `byteOffsetStart` +
 `arrayByteLength`.
8. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): A 0-length
 [TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
 whose `[[ByteOffset]]` is
 `bufferByteLength` is not considered out-of-bounds.
9. If `byteOffsetStart` \> `bufferByteLength` or
 `byteOffsetEnd` \> `bufferByteLength`, return
 true.
10. Return false.

# [10.4.5.15] IsTypedArrayFixedLength ( `obj` )

The abstract operation IsTypedArrayFixedLength takes argument
`obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray))
and returns a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `obj`.`[[ArrayLength]]` is auto,
 return false.
2. Let `buffer` be
 `obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`.
3. If
 [IsFixedLengthArrayBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-isfixedlengtharraybuffer)(`buffer`)
 is false and
 [IsSharedArrayBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-issharedarraybuffer)(`buffer`)
 is false, return false.
4. Return true.

# [10.4.5.16] IsValidIntegerIndex ( `obj`, `index` )

The abstract operation IsValidIntegerIndex takes arguments
`obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray))
and `index` (a Number) and returns a Boolean. It performs the
following steps when called:

1. If
 [IsDetachedBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-isdetachedbuffer)(`obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`) is true, return false.
2. If `index` is not an [integral
 Number](notational-conventions.html#integral-number), return false.
3. If `index` is -0~𝔽~ or `index` \< -0~𝔽~,
 return false.
4. Let `taRecord` be
 [MakeTypedArrayWithBufferWitnessRecord](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-maketypedarraywithbufferwitnessrecord)(`obj`,
 unordered).
5. [NOTE](notational-conventions.html#note-step): Bounds checking is
 not a synchronizing operation when `obj`\'s backing
 buffer is a [growable
 SharedArrayBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-fixed-length-and-growable-sharedarraybuffer-objects).
6. If
 [IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayoutofbounds)(`taRecord`)
 is true, return false.
7. Let `length` be
 [TypedArrayLength](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-typedarraylength)(`taRecord`).
8. If [ℝ](notational-conventions.html#%E2%84%9D)(`index`) ≥
 `length`, return false.
9. Return true.

# [10.4.5.17] TypedArrayGetElement ( `obj`, `index` )

The abstract operation TypedArrayGetElement takes arguments
`obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray))
and `index` (a Number) and returns a Number, a BigInt, or
undefined. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `index`) is false, return undefined.
2. Let `offset` be
 `obj`.`[[ByteOffset]]`.
3. Let `elementSize` be
 [TypedArrayElementSize](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementsize)(`obj`).
4. Let `byteIndexInBuffer` be
 ([ℝ](notational-conventions.html#%E2%84%9D)(`index`) ×
 `elementSize`) + `offset`.
5. Let `elementType` be
 [TypedArrayElementType](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementtype)(`obj`).
6. Return
 [GetValueFromBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-getvaluefrombuffer)(`obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`, `byteIndexInBuffer`, `elementType`,
 true, unordered).

# [10.4.5.18] TypedArraySetElement ( `obj`, `index`, `value` )

The abstract operation TypedArraySetElement takes arguments
`obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)),
`index` (a Number), and `value` (an [ECMAScript
language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
unused or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `obj`.`[[ContentType]]` is bigint,
 let `number` be
 ? [ToBigInt](abstract-operations.html#sec-tobigint)(`value`).
2. Else, let `number` be
 ? [ToNumber](abstract-operations.html#sec-tonumber)(`value`).
3. If
 [IsValidIntegerIndex](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-isvalidintegerindex)(`obj`,
 `index`) is true, then
 1. Let `offset` be
 `obj`.`[[ByteOffset]]`.
 2. Let `elementSize` be
 [TypedArrayElementSize](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementsize)(`obj`).
 3. Let `byteIndexInBuffer` be
 ([ℝ](notational-conventions.html#%E2%84%9D)(`index`)
 × `elementSize`) + `offset`.
 4. Let `elementType` be
 [TypedArrayElementType](indexed-collections.html#sec-typedarrayelementtype)(`obj`).
 5. Perform
 [SetValueInBuffer](structured-data.html#sec-setvalueinbuffer)(`obj`.`[[ViewedArrayBuffer]]`, `byteIndexInBuffer`,
 `elementType`, `number`, true, unordered).
4. Return unused.

[Note]

::: note-contents
This operation always appears to succeed, but it has no effect when
attempting to write past the end of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray) or
to a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)
which is backed by a detached ArrayBuffer.

# [10.4.5.19] IsArrayBufferViewOutOfBounds ( `obj` )

The abstract operation IsArrayBufferViewOutOfBounds takes argument
`obj` (a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray) or
a DataView) and returns a Boolean. It checks if either any of a
[TypedArray](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#typedarray)\'s
numeric properties or a DataView object\'s
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) can reference a
value at an index not contained within the underlying data block\'s
bounds. This abstract operation exists as a convenience for upstream
specifications. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `obj` has a `[[DataView]]` internal
 slot, then
 1. Let `viewRecord` be
 [MakeDataViewWithBufferWitnessRecord](structured-data.html#sec-makedataviewwithbufferwitnessrecord)(`obj`,
 seq-cst).
 2. Return
 [IsViewOutOfBounds](structured-data.html#sec-isviewoutofbounds)(`viewRecord`).
2. Let `taRecord` be
 [MakeTypedArrayWithBufferWitnessRecord](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-maketypedarraywithbufferwitnessrecord)(`obj`,
 seq-cst).
3. Return
 [IsTypedArrayOutOfBounds](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-istypedarrayoutofbounds)(`taRecord`).

# [10.4.6] Module Namespace Exotic Objects

A [module namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
is an [exotic
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object) that
exposes the bindings exported from an ECMAScript
[Module](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#prod-Module) (See
[16.2.3](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#sec-exports)).
There is a one-to-one correspondence between the String-keyed [own
properties](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of a
[module namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
and the binding names exported by the
[Module](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#prod-Module). The
exported bindings include any bindings that are indirectly exported
using `export *` export items. Each String-valued [own
property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) key is the
[StringValue](ecmascript-language-expressions.html#sec-static-semantics-stringvalue)
of the corresponding exported binding name. These are the only
String-keyed properties of a [module namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object).
Each such property has the
[attributes](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute) {
`[[Writable]]`: true, `[[Enumerable]]`: true, `[[Configurable]]`: false }. [Module
namespace exotic
objects](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
are not extensible.

An object is a [module namespace exotic
object] if its
`[[GetPrototypeOf]]`, `[[SetPrototypeOf]]`, `[[IsExtensible]]`,
`[[PreventExtensions]]`,
`[[GetOwnProperty]]`,
`[[DefineOwnProperty]]`, `[[HasProperty]]`, `[[Get]]`, `[[Set]]`,
`[[Delete]]`, and `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` internal [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method)
use the definitions in this section, and its other essential internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) use the
definitions found in
[10.1](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinary-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots).
These [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) are
installed by
[ModuleNamespaceCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-modulenamespacecreate).

[Module namespace exotic
objects](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
have the internal slots defined in [Table
29](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-internal-slots-of-module-namespace-exotic-objects).

<figure>
<span id="table-29"></span>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Internal Slot</th>
<th>Type</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[Module]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#sec-abstract-module-records">Module
Record</a></td>
<td>The <a
href="ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#sec-abstract-module-records">Module
Record</a> whose exports this namespace exposes.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><var class="field">[[Exports]]</var></td>
<td>a <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a>
of Strings</td>
<td>A <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type">List</a>
whose elements are the String values of the exported names exposed as <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property">own
properties</a> of this object. The list is sorted according to <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#lexicographic-code-unit-order">lexicographic
code unit order</a>.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption>Table 29: Internal Slots of Module Namespace Exotic
Objects</figcaption>
</figure>

# [10.4.6.1] `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` ( )

The `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
null. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return null.

# [10.4.6.2] `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` ( `proto` )

The `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ! [SetImmutablePrototype](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-set-immutable-prototype)(`obj`,
 `proto`).

# [10.4.6.3] `[[IsExtensible]]` ( )

The `[[IsExtensible]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
false. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return false.

# [10.4.6.4] `[[PreventExtensions]]` ( )

The `[[PreventExtensions]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
true. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return true.

# [10.4.6.5] `[[GetOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[GetOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined, or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 return
 [OrdinaryGetOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarygetownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).
2. Let `exports` be `obj`.`[[Exports]]`.
3. If `exports` does not contain `propertyKey`,
 return undefined.
4. Let `value` be
 ? `obj`.[\[\[Get\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-get)(`propertyKey`,
 `obj`).
5. Return PropertyDescriptor { `[[Value]]`:
 `value`, `[[Writable]]`: true,
 `[[Enumerable]]`: true,
 `[[Configurable]]`: false }.

# [10.4.6.6] `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc` )

The `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`propertyDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 return
 ! [OrdinaryDefineOwnProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydefineownproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc`).
2. Let `current` be
 ? `obj`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`).
3. If `current` is undefined, return false.
4. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Configurable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 true, return false.
5. If `propertyDesc` has an `[[Enumerable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Enumerable]]` is
 false, return false.
6. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`propertyDesc`)
 is true, return false.
7. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Writable]]`
 field and `propertyDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 is false, return false.
8. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Value]]`
 field, return
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`propertyDesc`.`[[Value]]`, `current`.`[[Value]]`).
9. Return true.

# [10.4.6.7] `[[HasProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[HasProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 return
 ! [OrdinaryHasProperty](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryhasproperty)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).
2. Let `exports` be `obj`.`[[Exports]]`.
3. If `exports` contains `propertyKey`, return
 true.
4. Return false.

# [10.4.6.8] `[[Get]]` ( `propertyKey`, `receiver` )

The `[[Get]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 then
 1. Return
 ! [OrdinaryGet](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryget)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`, `receiver`).
2. Let `exports` be `obj`.`[[Exports]]`.
3. If `exports` does not contain `propertyKey`,
 return undefined.
4. Let `module` be `obj`.`[[Module]]`.
5. Let `binding` be
 `module`.[ResolveExport](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#abstract-resolveexport)(`propertyKey`).
6. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `binding`
 is a [ResolvedBinding
 Record](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#resolvedbinding-record).
7. Let `targetModule` be
 `binding`.`[[Module]]`.
8. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `targetModule` is not undefined.
9. If `binding`.`[[BindingName]]` is
 namespace, then
 1. Return
 [GetModuleNamespace](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#sec-getmodulenamespace)(`targetModule`).
10. Let `targetEnv` be
 `targetModule`.`[[Environment]]`.
11. If `targetEnv` is empty, throw a ReferenceError
 exception.
12. Return
 ? `targetEnv`.[GetBindingValue](executable-code-and-execution-contexts.html#abstract-getbindingvalue)(`binding`.`[[BindingName]]`, true).

[Note]

::: note-contents
[ResolveExport](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#abstract-resolveexport)
is side-effect free. Each time this operation is called with a specific
`exportName`, `resolveSet` pair as arguments it
must return the same result. An implementation might choose to
pre-compute or cache the
[ResolveExport](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#abstract-resolveexport)
results for the `[[Exports]]` of each [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object).

# [10.4.6.9] `[[Set]]` ( `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver` )

The `[[Set]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
false. It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return false.

# [10.4.6.10] `[[Delete]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[Delete]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns a
[normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean. It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `propertyKey` [is a
 Symbol](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-symbol-type),
 then
 1. Return
 ! [OrdinaryDelete](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinarydelete)(`obj`,
 `propertyKey`).
2. Let `exports` be `obj`.`[[Exports]]`.
3. If `exports` contains `propertyKey`, return
 false.
4. Return true.

# [10.4.6.11] `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` ( )

The `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [module
namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [property keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `exports` be `obj`.`[[Exports]]`.
2. Let `symbolKeys` be
 [OrdinaryOwnPropertyKeys](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-ordinaryownpropertykeys)(`obj`).
3. Return the
 [list-concatenation](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#list-concatenation)
 of `exports` and `symbolKeys`.

# [10.4.6.12] ModuleNamespaceCreate ( `module`, `exports` )

The abstract operation ModuleNamespaceCreate takes arguments
`module` (a [Module
Record](ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#sec-abstract-module-records))
and `exports` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of Strings) and returns a [module namespace exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object).
It is used to specify the creation of new [module namespace exotic
objects](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#module-namespace-exotic-object).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `module`.`[[Namespace]]` is empty.
2. Let `internalSlotsList` be the internal slots listed in
 [Table
 29](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-internal-slots-of-module-namespace-exotic-objects).
3. Let `namespace` be
 [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)(`internalSlotsList`).
4. Set `namespace`\'s essential internal
 [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) to the
 definitions specified in
 [10.4.6](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-module-namespace-exotic-objects).
5. Set `namespace`.`[[Module]]` to
 `module`.
6. Let `sortedExports` be a
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 whose elements are the elements of `exports`, sorted
 according to [lexicographic code unit
 order](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#lexicographic-code-unit-order).
7. Set `namespace`.`[[Exports]]` to
 `sortedExports`.
8. Create [own
 properties](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of
 `namespace` corresponding to the definitions in
 [28.3](reflection.html#sec-module-namespace-objects).
9. Set `module`.`[[Namespace]]` to
 `namespace`.
10. Return `namespace`.

# [10.4.7] Immutable Prototype Exotic Objects

An [immutable prototype exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#immutable-prototype-exotic-object)
is an [exotic
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object) that has a
`[[Prototype]]` internal slot that will not change
once it is initialized.

An object is an [immutable prototype exotic
object] if its
`[[SetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) uses the
following implementation. (Its other essential internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) may use any
implementation, depending on the specific [immutable prototype exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#immutable-prototype-exotic-object)
in question.)

[Note]

::: note-contents
Unlike other [exotic
objects](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object), there is
not a dedicated creation abstract operation provided for [immutable
prototype exotic
objects](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#immutable-prototype-exotic-object).
This is because they are only used by
[%Object.prototype%](fundamental-objects.html#sec-properties-of-the-object-prototype-object)
and by [host environments](overview.html#host-environment), and in [host
environments](overview.html#host-environment), the relevant objects are
potentially exotic in other ways and thus need their own dedicated
creation operation.

# [10.4.7.1] `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` ( `proto` )

The `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of an [immutable
prototype exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#immutable-prototype-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Return
 ? [SetImmutablePrototype](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-set-immutable-prototype)(`obj`,
 `proto`).

# [10.4.7.2] SetImmutablePrototype ( `obj`, `proto` )

The abstract operation SetImmutablePrototype takes arguments
`obj` (an Object) and `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Let `current` be
 ? [`obj`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
2. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`proto`,
 `current`) is true, return true.
3. Return false.

# [10.5] Proxy Object Internal Methods and Internal Slots

A Proxy object is an [exotic
object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#exotic-object) whose
essential internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) are partially
implemented using ECMAScript code. Every Proxy object has an internal
slot called `[[ProxyHandler]]`. The value of
`[[ProxyHandler]]` is an object, called the proxy\'s
*handler object*, or null.
[Methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) (see [Table
30](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#table-proxy-handler-methods))
of a handler object may be used to augment the implementation for one or
more of the Proxy object\'s internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method). Every Proxy
object also has an internal slot called `[[ProxyTarget]]` whose value is either an object or null. This object is called
the proxy\'s *target object*.

An object is a [Proxy exotic object] if its essential internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) (including
`[[Call]]` and `[[Construct]]`, if
applicable) use the definitions in this section. These internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) are installed in
[ProxyCreate](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-proxycreate).

<figure>

</tr>
</thead>

</tr>

<td><code>preventExtensions</code></td>
</tr>

</tr>
