<figcaption>Table 30: Proxy Handler <a
href="ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method">Methods</a></figcaption>
</figure>

When a handler [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) is
called to provide the implementation of a Proxy object internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method), the handler
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) is passed the
proxy\'s target object as a parameter. A proxy\'s handler object does
not necessarily have a
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) corresponding to
every essential internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method). Invoking an
internal [method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) on the
proxy results in the invocation of the corresponding internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) on the proxy\'s
target object if the handler object does not have a
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) corresponding to
the internal trap.

The `[[ProxyHandler]]` and `[[ProxyTarget]]` internal slots of a Proxy object are always initialized when the
object is created and typically may not be modified. Some Proxy objects
are created in a manner that permits them to be subsequently *revoked*.
When a proxy is revoked, its `[[ProxyHandler]]` and
`[[ProxyTarget]]` internal slots are set to null
causing subsequent invocations of internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) on that Proxy
object to throw a TypeError exception.

Because Proxy objects permit the implementation of internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) to be provided
by arbitrary ECMAScript code, it is possible to define a Proxy object
whose handler [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method)
violates the invariants defined in
[6.1.7.3](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods).
Some of the internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) invariants
defined in
[6.1.7.3](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-invariants-of-the-essential-internal-methods)
are essential integrity invariants. These invariants are explicitly
enforced by the Proxy object internal
[methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) specified in
this section. An ECMAScript implementation must be robust in the
presence of all possible invariant violations.

In the following algorithm descriptions, assume `obj` is an
ECMAScript Proxy object, `propertyKey` is a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key) value,
`value` is any [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
and `propertyDesc` is a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
record.

# [10.5.1] `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` ( )

The `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns either a [normal
completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either an Object or null, or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"getPrototypeOf\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
7. Let `handlerProto` be
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target` »).
8. If `handlerProto` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) and
 `handlerProto` is not null, throw a TypeError exception.
9. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
10. If `extensibleTarget` is true, return
 `handlerProto`.
11. Let `targetProto` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
12. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`handlerProto`,
 `targetProto`) is false, throw a TypeError exception.
13. Return `handlerProto`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[GetPrototypeOf]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` must be either an
 Object or null.
- If the target object is not extensible, `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` applied to the Proxy object must return the same value as
 `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` applied to the Proxy object\'s
 target object.

# [10.5.2] `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` ( `proto` )

The `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `proto` (an Object or null)
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"setPrototypeOf\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[SetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-setprototypeof)(`proto`)].
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`, `proto` »)).
8. If `boolTrapResult` is false, return false.
9. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
10. If `extensibleTarget` is true, return true.
11. Let `targetProto` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetPrototypeOf\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getprototypeof)()].
12. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`proto`,
 `targetProto`) is false, throw a TypeError exception.
13. Return true.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[SetPrototypeOf]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[SetPrototypeOf]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- If the target object is not extensible, the argument value must be the
 same as the result of `[[GetPrototypeOf]]` applied
 to target object.

# [10.5.3] `[[IsExtensible]]` ( )

The `[[IsExtensible]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns either a [normal
completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"isExtensible\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target` »)).
8. Let `targetResult` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
9. If `boolTrapResult` is not `targetResult`,
 throw a TypeError exception.
10. Return `boolTrapResult`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[IsExtensible]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[IsExtensible]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- `[[IsExtensible]]` applied to the Proxy object must
 return the same value as `[[IsExtensible]]` applied
 to the Proxy object\'s target object with the same argument.

# [10.5.4] `[[PreventExtensions]]` ( )

The `[[PreventExtensions]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns either a [normal
completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"preventExtensions\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[PreventExtensions\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-preventextensions)()].
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target` »)).
8. If `boolTrapResult` is true, then
 1. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
 2. If `extensibleTarget` is true, throw a TypeError
 exception.
9. Return `boolTrapResult`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[PreventExtensions]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[PreventExtensions]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- `[[PreventExtensions]]` applied to the Proxy object
 only returns true if `[[IsExtensible]]` applied to
 the Proxy object\'s target object is false.

# [10.5.5] `[[GetOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[GetOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
either a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
or undefined, or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"getOwnPropertyDescriptor\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
7. Let `trapResultObj` be
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey` »).
8. If `trapResultObj` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) and
 `trapResultObj` is not undefined, throw a TypeError
 exception.
9. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
10. If `trapResultObj` is undefined, then
 1. If `targetDesc` is undefined, return undefined.
 2. If `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`
 is false, throw a TypeError exception.
 3. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
 4. If `extensibleTarget` is false, throw a TypeError
 exception.
 5. Return undefined.
11. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
12. Let `resultDesc` be
 ? [ToPropertyDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-topropertydescriptor)(`trapResultObj`).
13. Perform
 [CompletePropertyDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completepropertydescriptor)(`resultDesc`).
14. Let `valid` be
 [IsCompatiblePropertyDescriptor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-iscompatiblepropertydescriptor)(`extensibleTarget`,
 `resultDesc`, `targetDesc`).
15. If `valid` is false, throw a TypeError exception.
16. If `resultDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, then
 1. If `targetDesc` is undefined or
 `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 true, then
 1. Throw a TypeError exception.
 2. If `resultDesc` has a `[[Writable]]` field and
 `resultDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is
 false, then
 1. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `targetDesc` has a `[[Writable]]` field.
 2. If `targetDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 is true, throw a TypeError exception.
17. Return `resultDesc`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[GetOwnProperty]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[GetOwnProperty]]` must be either a
 [Property
 Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
 or undefined.
- A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as a
 non-configurable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as an [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of a
 non-extensible target object.
- A property cannot be reported as existent, if it does not exist as an
 [own property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of
 the target object and the target object is not extensible.
- A property cannot be reported as non-configurable, unless it exists as
 a non-configurable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- A property cannot be reported as both non-configurable and
 non-writable, unless it exists as a non-configurable, non-writable
 [own property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of
 the target object.

# [10.5.6] `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` ( `propertyKey`, `propertyDesc` )

The `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`propertyDesc` (a [Property
Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"defineProperty\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[DefineOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-defineownproperty)(`propertyKey`,
 `propertyDesc`)].
7. Let `propertyDescObj` be
 [FromPropertyDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-frompropertydescriptor)(`propertyDesc`).
8. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey`, `propertyDescObj` »)).
9. If `boolTrapResult` is false, return false.
10. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
11. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
12. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Configurable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, then
 1. Let `settingConfigurableFalse` be true.
13. Else,
 1. Let `settingConfigurableFalse` be false.
14. If `targetDesc` is undefined, then
 1. If `extensibleTarget` is false, throw a TypeError
 exception.
 2. If `settingConfigurableFalse` is true, throw a
 TypeError exception.
15. Else,
 1. If
 [IsCompatiblePropertyDescriptor](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-iscompatiblepropertydescriptor)(`extensibleTarget`,
 `propertyDesc`, `targetDesc`) is false,
 throw a TypeError exception.
 2. If `settingConfigurableFalse` is true and
 `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 true, throw a TypeError exception.
 3. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`targetDesc`)
 is true, `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is false, and
 `targetDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is
 true, then
 1. If `propertyDesc` has a `[[Writable]]` field and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Writable]]`
 is false, throw a TypeError exception.
16. Return true.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[DefineOwnProperty]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- A property cannot be added, if the target object is not extensible.
- A property cannot be non-configurable, unless there exists a
 corresponding non-configurable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- A non-configurable property cannot be non-writable, unless there
 exists a corresponding non-configurable, non-writable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- If a property has a corresponding target object property then applying
 the [Property
 Descriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-property-descriptor-specification-type)
 of the property to the target object using
 `[[DefineOwnProperty]]` will not throw an exception.

# [10.5.7] `[[HasProperty]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[HasProperty]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"has\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[HasProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-hasproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey` »)).
8. If `boolTrapResult` is false, then
 1. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
 2. If `targetDesc` is not undefined, then
 1. If `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is false, throw a TypeError exception.
 2. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
 3. If `extensibleTarget` is false, throw a TypeError
 exception.
9. Return `boolTrapResult`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[HasProperty]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[HasProperty]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as a
 non-configurable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- A property cannot be reported as non-existent, if it exists as an [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object and the target object is not extensible.

# [10.5.8] `[[Get]]` ( `propertyKey`, `receiver` )

The `[[Get]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and
`receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"get\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[Get\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-get)(`propertyKey`,
 `receiver`)].
7. Let `trapResult` be
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey`, `receiver` »).
8. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
9. If `targetDesc` is not undefined and
 `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, then
 1. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`targetDesc`)
 is true and `targetDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is false, then
 1. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`trapResult`,
 `targetDesc`.`[[Value]]`) is
 false, throw a TypeError exception.
 2. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`targetDesc`)
 is true and `targetDesc`.`[[Getter]]` is undefined, then
 1. If `trapResult` is not undefined, throw a
 TypeError exception.
10. Return `trapResult`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[Get]]` for Proxy objects enforces the following
invariants:

- The value reported for a property must be the same as the value of the
 corresponding target object property if the target object property is
 a non-writable, non-configurable own [data
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
- The value reported for a property must be undefined if the
 corresponding target object property is a non-configurable own
 [accessor
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) that
 has undefined as its `[[Getter]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).

# [10.5.9] `[[Set]]` ( `propertyKey`, `value`, `receiver` )

The `[[Set]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)),
`value` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)),
and `receiver` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"set\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[Set\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-set)(`propertyKey`,
 `value`, `receiver`)].
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey`, `value`,
 `receiver` »)).
8. If `boolTrapResult` is false, return false.
9. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
10. If `targetDesc` is not undefined and
 `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, then
 1. If
 [IsDataDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isdatadescriptor)(`targetDesc`)
 is true and `targetDesc`.`[[Writable]]` is false, then
 1. If
 [SameValue](abstract-operations.html#sec-samevalue)(`value`,
 `targetDesc`.`[[Value]]`) is
 false, throw a TypeError exception.
 2. If
 [IsAccessorDescriptor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-isaccessordescriptor)(`targetDesc`)
 is true, then
 1. If `targetDesc`.`[[Setter]]` is
 undefined, throw a TypeError exception.
11. Return true.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[Set]]` for Proxy objects enforces the following
invariants:

- The result of `[[Set]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- Cannot change the value of a property to be different from the value
 of the corresponding target object property if the corresponding
 target object property is a non-writable, non-configurable own [data
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
- Cannot set the value of a property if the corresponding target object
 property is a non-configurable own [accessor
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type) that
 has undefined as its `[[Setter]]`
 [attribute](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#attribute).

# [10.5.10] `[[Delete]]` ( `propertyKey` )

The `[[Delete]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes argument `propertyKey` (a [property
key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)) and returns
either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a Boolean or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"deleteProperty\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[Delete\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-delete)(`propertyKey`)].
7. Let `boolTrapResult` be
 [ToBoolean](abstract-operations.html#sec-toboolean)(?
 [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`,
 `propertyKey` »)).
8. If `boolTrapResult` is false, return false.
9. Let `targetDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`propertyKey`)].
10. If `targetDesc` is undefined, return true.
11. If `targetDesc`.`[[Configurable]]` is
 false, throw a TypeError exception.
12. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
13. If `extensibleTarget` is false, throw a TypeError
 exception.
14. Return true.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[Delete]]` for Proxy objects enforces the following
invariants:

- The result of `[[Delete]]` [is a
 Boolean](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types-boolean-type)
 value.
- A property cannot be reported as deleted, if it exists as a
 non-configurable [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- A property cannot be reported as deleted, if it exists as an [own
 property](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object and the target object is non-extensible.

# [10.5.11] `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` ( )

The `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes no arguments and returns either a [normal
completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [property keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"ownKeys\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [`target`.[\[\[OwnPropertyKeys\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-ownpropertykeys)()].
7. Let `trapResultArray` be
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target` »).
8. Let `trapResult` be
 ? [CreateListFromArrayLike](abstract-operations.html#sec-createlistfromarraylike)(`trapResultArray`,
 property-key).
9. If `trapResult` contains any duplicate entries, throw a
 TypeError exception.
10. Let `extensibleTarget` be
 ? [IsExtensible](abstract-operations.html#sec-isextensible-o)(`target`).
11. Let `targetKeys` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[OwnPropertyKeys\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-ownpropertykeys)()].
12. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `targetKeys` is a
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 of [property
 keys](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
13. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `targetKeys` contains no duplicate entries.
14. Let `targetConfigurableKeys` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
15. Let `targetNonconfigurableKeys` be a new empty
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
16. For each element `key` of `targetKeys`, do
 1. Let `propertyDesc` be
 ? [`target`.[\[\[GetOwnProperty\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-getownproperty)(`key`)].
 2. If `propertyDesc` is not undefined and
 `propertyDesc`.`[[Configurable]]`
 is false, then
 1. Append `key` to
 `targetNonconfigurableKeys`.
 3. Else,
 1. Append `key` to
 `targetConfigurableKeys`.
17. If `extensibleTarget` is true and
 `targetNonconfigurableKeys` is empty, then
 1. Return `trapResult`.
18. Let `uncheckedResultKeys` be a
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 whose elements are the elements of `trapResult`.
19. For each element `key` of
 `targetNonconfigurableKeys`, do
 1. If `uncheckedResultKeys` does not contain
 `key`, throw a TypeError exception.
 2. Remove `key` from `uncheckedResultKeys`.
20. If `extensibleTarget` is true, return
 `trapResult`.
21. For each element `key` of
 `targetConfigurableKeys`, do
 1. If `uncheckedResultKeys` does not contain
 `key`, throw a TypeError exception.
 2. Remove `key` from `uncheckedResultKeys`.
22. If `uncheckedResultKeys` is not empty, throw a TypeError
 exception.
23. Return `trapResult`.

[Note]

::: note-contents
`[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[OwnPropertyKeys]]` is a
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type).
- The returned
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 contains no duplicate entries.
- Each element of the returned
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 is a [property
 key](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#property-key).
- The result
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 must contain the keys of all non-configurable [own
 properties](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object.
- If the target object is not extensible, then the result
 [List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
 must contain all the keys of the [own
 properties](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#own-property) of the
 target object and no other values.

# [10.5.12] `[[Call]]` ( `thisArg`, `argList` )

The `[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `thisArg` (an [ECMAScript
language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
4. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
5. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"apply\").
6. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`target`,
 `thisArg`, `argList`).
7. Let `argArray` be
 [CreateArrayFromList](abstract-operations.html#sec-createarrayfromlist)(`argList`).
8. Return
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`, `thisArg`,
 `argArray` »).

[Note]

::: note-contents
A [Proxy exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
only has a `[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) if the initial
value of its `[[ProxyTarget]]` internal slot is an
object that has a `[[Call]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method).

# [10.5.13] `[[Construct]]` ( `argList`, `newTarget` )

The `[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) of a [Proxy
exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
`obj` takes arguments `argList` (a
[List](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-list-and-record-specification-type)
of [ECMAScript language
values](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `newTarget` (a
[constructor](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#constructor)) and
returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
an Object or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It performs the following steps when called:

1. Perform
 ? [ValidateNonRevokedProxy](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-validatenonrevokedproxy)(`obj`).
2. Let `target` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyTarget]]`.
3. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 [IsConstructor](abstract-operations.html#sec-isconstructor)(`target`)
 is true.
4. Let `handler` be
 `obj`.`[[ProxyHandler]]`.
5. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert): `handler`
 [is an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type).
6. Let `trap` be
 ? [GetMethod](abstract-operations.html#sec-getmethod)(`handler`,
 \"construct\").
7. If `trap` is undefined, then
 1. Return
 ? [Construct](abstract-operations.html#sec-construct)(`target`,
 `argList`, `newTarget`).
8. Let `argArray` be
 [CreateArrayFromList](abstract-operations.html#sec-createarrayfromlist)(`argList`).
9. Let `newObj` be
 ? [Call](abstract-operations.html#sec-call)(`trap`,
 `handler`, « `target`, `argArray`,
 `newTarget` »).
10. If `newObj` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 throw a TypeError exception.
11. Return `newObj`.

[Note 1]

::: note-contents
A [Proxy exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
only has a `[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method) if the initial
value of its `[[ProxyTarget]]` internal slot is an
object that has a `[[Construct]]` internal
[method](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method).

[Note 2]

::: note-contents
`[[Construct]]` for Proxy objects enforces the
following invariants:

- The result of `[[Construct]]` must be an Object.

# [10.5.14] ValidateNonRevokedProxy ( `proxy` )

The abstract operation ValidateNonRevokedProxy takes argument
`proxy` (a [Proxy exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
unused or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It throws a TypeError exception if `proxy` has been revoked.
It performs the following steps when called:

1. If `proxy`.`[[ProxyTarget]]` is null,
 throw a TypeError exception.
2. [Assert](notational-conventions.html#assert):
 `proxy`.`[[ProxyHandler]]` is not null.
3. Return unused.

# [10.5.15] ProxyCreate ( `target`, `handler` )

The abstract operation ProxyCreate takes arguments `target`
(an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and `handler` (an [ECMAScript language
value](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-ecmascript-language-types))
and returns either a [normal completion
containing](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type)
a [Proxy exotic
object](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#proxy-exotic-object)
or a [throw
completion](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-completion-record-specification-type).
It is used to specify the creation of new Proxy objects. It performs the
following steps when called:

1. If `target` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 throw a TypeError exception.
2. If `handler` [is not an
 Object](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#sec-object-type),
 throw a TypeError exception.
3. Let `proxy` be
 [MakeBasicObject](abstract-operations.html#sec-makebasicobject)(«
 `[[ProxyHandler]]`, `[[ProxyTarget]]` »).
4. Set `proxy`\'s essential internal
 [methods](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#method), except for
 `[[Call]]` and `[[Construct]]`,
 to the definitions specified in
 [10.5](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-proxy-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots).
5. If
 [IsCallable](abstract-operations.html#sec-iscallable)(`target`)
 is true, then
 1. Set
 `proxy`.[\[\[Call\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-call)
 as specified in
 [10.5.12](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-proxy-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots-call-thisargument-argumentslist).
 2. If
 [IsConstructor](abstract-operations.html#sec-isconstructor)(`target`)
 is true, then
 1. Set
 `proxy`.[\[\[Construct\]\]](ecmascript-data-types-and-values.html#internal-method-construct)
 as specified in
 [10.5.13](ordinary-and-exotic-objects-behaviours.html#sec-proxy-object-internal-methods-and-internal-slots-construct-argumentslist-newtarget).
6. Set `proxy`.`[[ProxyTarget]]` to
 `target`.
7. Set `proxy`.`[[ProxyHandler]]` to
 `handler`.
8. Return `proxy`.
